     4	enum PagePins {
     5	    Plain(Vec<String>),
     6	    Product { system: Vec<String>, branches: Vec<Vec<String>> },
     7	    Invalid,
     8	}
     9	
    10	/// Reconstruct the native planner's bounded Cartesian product from the
    11	/// authored filter.  This stays surface-derived: the executable model cannot
    12	/// trust the binder's proof metadata when deciding whether comparison is safe.
    13	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — every modeled PAGE filter lane has its own exact physical prefix.
    14	fn page_pins(table: &Table, read: &ReadExpr) -> PagePins {
    15	    let Some(filter) = read.filter.as_deref() else { return PagePins::Plain(Vec::new()) };
    16	    let mut conjuncts = Vec::new();
    17	    flatten_and(filter, &mut conjuncts);
    18	    let system = model_pin_columns(table, &conjuncts, &read.order_keys);
    19	    let mut branches = vec![Vec::new()];
    20	    let mut distributed = false;
    21	    for conjunct in &conjuncts {
    22	        let mut alternatives = Vec::new();
    23	        flatten_or(conjunct, &mut alternatives);
    24	        distributed |= alternatives.len() > 1;
    25	        if branches.len().saturating_mul(alternatives.len()) > 8 { return PagePins::Invalid; }
    26	        branches = branches.into_iter().flat_map(|prefix| alternatives.iter().map(move |alternative| {
    27	            let mut lane = prefix.clone();
    28	            flatten_and(alternative, &mut lane);
    29	            lane
    30	        })).collect();
    31	    }
    32	    if !distributed || branches.len() < 2 { return PagePins::Plain(system); }
    33	    if branches.iter().any(|branch| !branch.iter().any(|atom| page_pin(table, atom))) {
    34	        return PagePins::Invalid;
    35	    }
    36	    PagePins::Product {
    37	        system,
    38	        branches: branches.into_iter().map(|branch| model_pin_columns(table, &branch, &read.order_keys)).collect(),
    39	    }
    40	}
    41	
    42	fn page_pin(table: &Table, expression: &Expr) -> bool {
    43	    let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = expression else { return false };
    44	    if op != "=" { return false; }
    45	    let (column, value) = if let Some(column) = column_of(left) { (column, right.as_ref()) }
    46	        else if let Some(column) = column_of(right) { (column, left.as_ref()) }
    47	        else { return false };
    48	    column != "id" && model_independent(value) && table.cols.iter().find(|field| field.name == column)
    49	        .is_some_and(|field| !field.nullable || matches!(value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer"))
    50	}
    51	
    52	/// The executable predicate classifier currently knows literal/viewer point
    53	/// terms only. Preserve an explicit boundary for admitted native argument
    54	/// and signed-expression keys rather than reporting a model disagreement
    55	/// under an incompletely translated completeness proof.
    56	pub(super) fn static_key_issue(read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    57	    fn missing(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
    58	        match expr {
    59	            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().all(|key| model_independent(&key.value))
    60	                && read.keys.iter().any(|key|
    61	                !matches!(&key.value, Expr::Literal { .. })
    62	                && !matches!(&key.value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer")),
    63	            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => missing(left) || missing(right),
    64	            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => missing(value),
    65	            _ => false,
    66	        }
    67	    }
    68	    read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(missing).then_some("page-static-key-expression")
    69	}
    70	
    71	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — reproduce implicit bounds from surface
    72	// constraints, independently of the planner's selected index or IR.
    73	pub(super) fn implicit_page(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    74	    if read.range.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.index.is_some() { return None; }
    75	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    76	    let mut predicates = Vec::new();
    77	    if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { flatten_and(filter, &mut predicates); }
    78	    let pinned: Vec<String> = predicates.iter().filter_map(|predicate| model_equality_column(target, predicate)).collect();
    79	    let cap = target.caps.iter().filter(|(_, by)| by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column)
    80	        && !target.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && col.on_delete == Some("setNull"))))
    81	        .map(|(max, _)| *max).chain(target.uniques.iter().filter(|key| key.iter().all(|column|
    82	            pinned.contains(column) && (column == "id" || target.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && !col.nullable))))
    83	            .map(|_| 1)).min()?;
    84	    if cap > 8192 { return None; }
    85	    let mut page = read.clone();
    86	    page.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: cap, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    87	    Some(page)
    88	}
    89	
    90	pub(super) fn membership_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    91	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None }
    92	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    93	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    94	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read && rule.bindings.is_empty()).map(|rule| &rule.value)?;
    95	    let mut leaves = Vec::new();
    96	    flatten_or(rule, &mut leaves);
    97	    let mut parties = Vec::new();
    98	    let mut memberships = Vec::new();
    99	    for leaf in leaves {
   100	        if let Some(column) = party_column(target, leaf) { parties.push(column.to_owned()); }
   101	        else if let Some(membership) = membership_physical(module, target, leaf) { memberships.push(membership); }
   102	        else { return None; }
   103	    }
   104	    if memberships.is_empty() { return None }
   105	    if memberships.iter().any(|membership| !membership.source_admissible) {
   106	        return Some("page-membership-source-unique");
   107	    }
   108	    let mut fits = Vec::new();
   109	    for membership in &memberships {
   110	        let Some(table) = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == membership.source) else { return Some("page-membership-key-bound") };
   111	        fits.push(match &membership.index {
   112	            Some(index) => model_selected_index_fit(module, table, index),
   113	            None => model_plain_index_fit(module, table, &membership.columns, false),
   114	        });
   115	    }
   116	    let (system_pins, branches) = match page_pins(target, read) {
   117	        PagePins::Plain(system) => (system.clone(), vec![system]),
   118	        PagePins::Product { system, branches } => (system, branches),
   119	        PagePins::Invalid => return Some("page-membership-filter-product"),
   120	    };
   121	    if 1usize.saturating_add(branches.len().saturating_mul(parties.len().saturating_add(memberships.len()))) > 8 {
   122	        return Some("page-membership-filter-product");
   123	    }
   124	    fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(system_pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   125	    for branch in branches {
   126	        for prefix in parties.iter().chain(memberships.iter().map(|membership| &membership.target_parent)) {
   127	            let mut pins = branch.clone();
   128	            if !pins.contains(prefix) { pins.push(prefix.clone()); pins.sort(); }
   129	            fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   130	        }
   131	    }
   132	    if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous) { Some("page-membership-index-selection") }
   133	    else if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Nonportable) { Some("page-membership-key-bound") }
   134	    else { None }
   135	}
   136	
   137	/// Compound PAGE3 remains outside the executable model when its physical
   138	/// lanes cannot be independently reconstructed from the surface. Logical
   139	/// distribution is proved separately; an unsupported read is never a pass.
   140	pub(super) fn compound_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
   141	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None; }
   142	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   143	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   144	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value.clone();
   145	    fn compound(e: &Expr) -> bool {
   146	        match e {
   147	            Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and" => true,
   148	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => compound(left) || compound(right),
   149	            _ => false,
   150	        }
   151	    }
   152	    if !compound(&rule) && !matches!(&rule, Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "or") { return None; }
   153	    fn ranges(e: &Expr) -> Option<Vec<Vec<&Expr>>> {
   154	        let out = match e {
   155	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
   156	                let mut out = ranges(left)?; out.extend(ranges(right)?); out
   157	            }
   158	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {
   159	                let a = ranges(left)?; let b = ranges(right)?;
   160	                if a.len().saturating_mul(b.len()) > 7 { return None; }
   161	                a.into_iter().flat_map(|a| b.iter().map(move |b| { let mut out = a.clone(); out.extend(b); out })).collect()
   162	            }
   163	            other => vec![vec![other]],
   164	        };
   165	        (out.len() <= 7).then_some(out)
   166	    }
   167	    let mut simple = Vec::new(); flatten_or(&rule, &mut simple);
   168	    // Existing party and exact point-membership classifiers take precedence;
   169	    // their where alternatives already have an independent physical proof.
   170	    if simple.iter().all(|leaf| party_column(target, leaf).is_some() || membership_physical(module, target, leaf).is_some()) { return None; }
   171	    let leaves = ranges(&rule)?;
   172	    let system = match page_pins(target, read) {
   173	        PagePins::Plain(system) => system,
   174	        PagePins::Product { .. } | PagePins::Invalid => return Some("page-compound-where-branches"),
   175	    };
   176	    // The model's compound classifier deliberately keeps the where-branch
   177	    // composition outside its fragment until it models each source prefix.
   178	    let mut fits = vec![model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(system.clone(), &read.order_keys), true)];
   179	    for leaf in leaves {
   180	        let mut pins = system.clone();
   181	        let mut predicates = Vec::new();
   182	        if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { flatten_and(filter, &mut predicates); }
   183	        predicates.extend(leaf.iter().copied());
   184	        let fixed: BTreeMap<_, _> = predicates.iter().filter_map(|atom| {
   185	            let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = atom else { return None };
   186	            if op != "=" { return None; }
   187	            let (column, value) = if let Some(column) = column_of(left) { (column, right.as_ref()) }
   188	                else { (column_of(right)?, left.as_ref()) };
   189	            matches!(value, Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. }).then(|| (column.to_owned(), value.clone()))
   190	        }).collect();
   191	        for atom in &leaf {
   192	            if let Some(column) = model_equality_column(target, atom) {
   193	                if target.cols.iter().any(|col| col.name == column && col.nullable)
   194	                    && party_column(target, atom).is_none() { return Some("page-compound-nullable-pin"); }
   195	                pins.push(column);
   196	            }
   197	        }
   198	        for atom in leaf {
   199	            let mut substituted = atom.clone();
   200	            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut substituted, &mut |e| {
   201	                if let Some(value) = column_of(e).and_then(|column| fixed.get(column)) { *e = value.clone(); }
   202	            });
   203	            if let Some(membership) = membership_physical(module, target, &substituted) {
   204	                if !pins.contains(&membership.target_parent) && !system.contains(&membership.target_parent) {
   205	                    if !membership.source_admissible { return Some("page-membership-source-unique"); }
   206	                    pins.push(membership.target_parent);
   207	                    let source = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == membership.source)?;
   208	                    fits.push(match &membership.index {
   209	                        Some(index) => model_selected_index_fit(module, source, index),
   210	                        None => model_plain_index_fit(module, source, &membership.columns, false),
   211	                    });
   212	                }
   213	            }
   214	        }
   215	        pins.sort(); pins.dedup();
   216	        fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   217	    }
   218	    if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous) { Some("page-compound-index-selection") }
   219	    else if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Nonportable) { Some("page-compound-key-bound") }
   220	    else { None }
   221	}
   222	
   223	
   224	/// The model's direct grant theorem remains positive. Standard bounded
   225	/// negative-grant pages use the independent branch classifier; direct scalar
   226	/// absence/aggregation and fallible-filter fallback stay explicitly outside
   227	/// that executable completeness fragment until its negative-view proof lands.
   228	pub(super) fn negative_grant_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
   229	    if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.filter.is_none() && read.range.is_none() { return None; }
   230	    fn negative_probe(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   231	        match expr {
   232	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op=="not" => matches!(value.as_ref(),Expr::Exists {..}),
   233	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" || op=="or" => negative_probe(left) || negative_probe(right),
   234	            _=>false,
   235	        }
   236	    }
   237	    let table = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   238	    let rule = &table.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value;
   239	    if !negative_probe(rule) { return None; }
   240	    fn total(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   241	        match expr {
   242	            Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } => true,
   243	            Expr::Member { base, .. } => matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }),
   244	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=" | "and" | "or" | "in") && total(left) && total(right),
   245	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => op == "not" && total(value),
   246	            Expr::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(total), _ => false,
   247	        }
   248	    }
   249	    fn distributed_width(expr: &Expr) -> usize {
   250	        match expr {
   251	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="or" => distributed_width(left).saturating_add(distributed_width(right)).min(8),
   252	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" => distributed_width(left).saturating_mul(distributed_width(right)).min(8),
   253	            _=>1,
   254	        }
   255	    }
   256	    (read.range.is_none() || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !total(filter)) || distributed_width(rule) >= 8)
   257	        .then_some("page-negative-grant-fallback")
   258	}
   259	
   260	/// Named order normalization is speculative in the native compiler. Only
   261	/// the independently modeled unfiltered mixed range fragment normalizes here;
   262	/// filtered or one-party fallback candidates stay named Unsupported.
   263	pub(super) fn named_merge_fragment(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   264	    if read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !matches!(filter, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), .. })) { return false; }
   265	    let Some(target)=module.tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==read.table) else {return false;};
   266	    let Some(rule)=module.surface_tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==read.table).and_then(|t|t.rules.iter().find(|r|r.effect==Effect::Read)).map(|r|&r.value) else {return false;};
   267	    if !matches!(rule,Expr::Binary {op,..} if op=="or") {return false;}
   268	    let mut leaves=Vec::new();flatten_or(rule,&mut leaves);
   269	    leaves.len()<=7 && leaves.iter().any(|leaf|party_column(target,leaf).is_some())
   270	        && leaves.iter().all(|leaf|party_column(target,leaf).is_some() || membership_physical(module,target,leaf).is_some()
   271	            || model_equality_column(target,leaf).is_some())
   272	}

   438	/// The branches of an undecided read, or `None` when the rule does not apply.
   439	fn branches(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &Requests, read: &ReadExpr, grants: bool) -> Option<Vec<Branch>> {
   440	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
   441	    if read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some() || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some() { return None; }
   442	    if table.maintained.is_some() || table.through.is_some() || table.decl.ephemeral.is_some() || !table.captures.is_empty() || table.decl.public.is_some() { return None; }
   443	    let rule = read_rule(table)?;
   444	    // A page its own pins decide keeps its plan.
   445	    let mut probe = requests.clone();
   446	    let mut planned = read.clone();
   447	    probe.read(tables, &mut planned, false);
   448	    if decided(tables, &planned) { return None; }
   449	    // Each lane evaluates the predicate's parts on its own rows: they must be
   450	    // total, so moving an alternative into its own lane cannot raise a refusal
   451	    // that the original's short circuit skipped (`or (… and 1 / n = 1)`).
   452	    if read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !super::intent_compound::total_readable(tables, synthesized, table, filter)) { return None; }
   453	    let conjuncts = read.filter.as_ref().map(|filter| { let mut out = Vec::new(); intent::split(*filter.clone(), &mut out); out }).unwrap_or_default();
   454	    let alternatives = where_branches(table, &conjuncts);
   455	    // Case 1: every alternative of the one `or` in `where` decides the rule.
   456	    if let Some(alternatives) = &alternatives
   457	        && alternatives.iter().all(|alternative| {
   458	            let mut lane = read.clone();
   459	            lane.filter = intent::conjunction(alternative.clone());
   460	            let mut probe = requests.clone();
   461	            probe.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane);
   462	            decided(tables, &lane)
   463	        }) {
   464	        return Some(alternatives.iter().map(|alternative| (alternative.clone(), false)).collect());
   465	    }
   466	    // Case 2: the read rule is parties and page guards: readable grants, and
   467	    // disjuncts the page's own pins decide (LLP 3000.036 §16.3), which the
   468	    // binding check confirms with the core's `covering_grant`.
   469	    let mut disjunct_list = Vec::new();
   470	    disjuncts(rule, &mut disjunct_list);
   471	    if disjunct_list.len() < 2 { return None; }
   472	    let pinned: Vec<String> = conjuncts.iter().filter_map(|conjunct| intent::equality(conjunct, table).map(|(column, _)| column)).collect();
   473	    let mut parties = Vec::new();
   474	    for disjunct in &disjunct_list {
   475	        if let Some(column) = party(table, disjunct) { if !parties.contains(&column) { parties.push(column); } }
   476	        else if columns_within(disjunct, &pinned) { /* a guard: the grant lane reads it */ }
   477	        else { return None; }
   478	    }
   479	    // `exists` takes case 1 only: an `or` of party reads misses rows for the
   480	    // system, which every rule admits, and a guard lane has no existence form.
   481	    if parties.is_empty() || !grants { return None; }
   482	    let base = alternatives.unwrap_or_else(|| vec![conjuncts.clone()]);
   483	    // The grant lane, the read as written, is read for a caller its guards
   484	    // admit and for the system (which every rule admits); a rule of parties
   485	    // alone has no guard, so the lane is the system's only.
   486	    let mut out = vec![(conjuncts, true)];
   487	    for branch in &base {
   488	        for column in &parties {
   489	            let mut lane = vec![viewer_pin(column, &read.site)];
   490	            lane.extend(branch.iter().cloned());
   491	            out.push((lane, false));
   492	        }
   493	    }
   494	    (out.len() <= MAX_BRANCHES).then_some(out)
   495	}
   496	
   497	/// The bounded positive alternatives of the read's top-level conjunction.
   498	/// Each `or` contributes a Cartesian factor, so
   499	/// `(a = x or b = x) and (state = 'open' or state = 'held')` becomes four
   500	/// independently pinned lanes.  Keep the published eight-lane ceiling while
   501	/// allowing an exact query predicate to narrow a wider read-rule union.
   502	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact positive query scopes may combine party and status alternatives.
   503	fn where_branches(table: &TableInfo, conjuncts: &[Expr]) -> Option<Vec<Vec<Expr>>> {
   504	    let mut branches = vec![Vec::new()];
   505	    let mut distributed = false;
   506	    for conjunct in conjuncts {
   507	        let mut alternatives = Vec::new();
   508	        disjuncts(conjunct, &mut alternatives);
   509	        distributed |= alternatives.len() > 1;
   510	        if branches.len().saturating_mul(alternatives.len()) > MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
   511	        branches = branches.into_iter().flat_map(|prefix| alternatives.iter().map(move |alternative| {
   512	            let mut lane = prefix.clone();
   513	            intent::split(alternative.clone(), &mut lane);
   514	            lane
   515	        })).collect();
   516	    }
   517	    if !distributed || branches.len() < 2 || branches.iter().any(|lane| !lane.iter().any(|conjunct| pin(table, conjunct))) {
   518	        return None;
   519	    }
   520	    Some(branches)
   521	}
   522	
   523	/// An equality the planner turns into an index prefix. A nullable column is
   524	/// pinned only to `viewer`, never to a value that may be null, so a branch
   525	/// never seeks a null the original filter rejects.
   526	fn pin(table: &TableInfo, conjunct: &Expr) -> bool {
   527	    let Some((column, value)) = intent::equality(conjunct, table) else { return false };
   528	    column != "id" && table.columns.get(&column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional || is_viewer(&value))
   529	}

   531	/// The planned read's prefix decides the read rule: every column the rule
   532	/// reads is pinned (with the self-witness proof), or the rule is true by the
   533	/// pins' form (the core's `rule_true`, and the Lean model's `Rule.byForm`).
   534	pub(super) fn decided(tables: &Tables, planned: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   535	    let Some(table) = tables.get(&planned.table) else { return false };
   536	    if super::intent_lanes::prefix_decides_rule(tables, planned) { return true; }
   537	    read_rule(table).is_some_and(|rule| by_form(rule, planned))
   538	}

   540	/// `allow`, `.col = viewer` pinned to `viewer`, `.col = 'x'` pinned to `'x'`,
   541	/// and `or`/`and` of those.
   542	pub(super) fn by_form(rule: &Expr, planned: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   543	    let pinned = |column: &str| planned.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_deref() == Some(column)).map(|key| &key.value);
   544	    match rule {
   545	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "allow" => true,
   546	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => by_form(left, planned) || by_form(right, planned),
   547	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => by_form(left, planned) && by_form(right, planned),
   548	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => {
   549	            let (column, value) = match (intent::column(left), intent::column(right)) {
   550	                (Some(column), None) => (column, right.as_ref()),
   551	                (None, Some(column)) => (column, left.as_ref()),
   552	                _ => return false,
   553	            };
   554	            match (value, pinned(column)) {
   555	                (value, Some(pin)) if is_viewer(value) => is_viewer(pin),
   556	                (Expr::Literal { value, .. }, Some(Expr::Literal { value: pin, .. })) => value == pin && *value != Literal::Null,
   557	                _ => false,
   558	            }
   559	        }
   560	        _ => false,
   561	    }
   562	}

   564	pub(super) fn read_rule(table: &TableInfo) -> Option<&Expr> {
   565	    match table.decl.rules.iter().filter(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read).collect::<Vec<_>>().as_slice() {
   566	        [rule] => Some(&rule.value),
   567	        _ => None,
   568	    }
   569	}

   572	    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value && op == "or" { disjuncts(left, out); disjuncts(right, out); }
   573	    else { out.push(value.clone()); }
   574	}

  1124	/// The physical index the native planner can reuse for a requested lane.
  1125	/// Its components must have the planner's exact sorted-prefix order. A
  1126	/// cursorless source may reuse a matching unique without its trailing `id`;
  1127	/// a cursored target needs that unique to be total.
  1128	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — the pre-oracle physical gate prices the native-compatible declared PAGE lane.
  1129	fn model_declared_indexes<'a>(module: &'a LeanModule, table: &Table, columns: &[String], cursor: bool) -> Vec<&'a ast::IndexDecl> {
  1130	    let Some(decl) = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|decl| decl.name == table.name) else { return Vec::new() };
  1131	    decl.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain() && (index.columns == columns
  1132	        || index.unique && (!cursor || model_total_unique(table, index)) && columns.last().map(String::as_str) == Some("id")
  1133	            && index.columns == columns[..columns.len() - 1])).collect()
  1134	}

  1136	fn model_index_label_fits(module: &LeanModule, table: &Table, columns: &[String], declared: Option<&ast::IndexDecl>) -> bool {
  1137	    let Some(decl) = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|decl| decl.name == table.name) else { return false };
  1138	    if let Some(index) = declared.or_else(|| decl.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns == columns).min_by_key(|index| &index.name)) {
  1139	        return table.name.len().saturating_add(index.name.len()) <= snapback4_core::rows::MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES;
  1140	    }
  1141	    let mut components = columns.to_vec();
  1142	    if components.len() > 1 && components.last().is_some_and(|column| column == "id") { components.pop(); }
  1143	    let base = format!("by{}", components.iter().map(|column| model_title(column)).collect::<String>());
  1144	    // `$intent:{base}:` plus the ordinary 16-byte digest suffix. A collision
  1145	    // can only lengthen the suffix, so a name already over budget cannot be a
  1146	    // physical PAGE lane on every store.
  1147	    let generated = "$intent:".len().saturating_add(base.len()).saturating_add(1 + 16);
  1148	    table.name.len().saturating_add(generated) <= snapback4_core::rows::MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES
  1149	}

  1151	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  1152	enum ModelIndexFit { Fits, Nonportable, Ambiguous }
  1153	
  1154	fn model_plain_index_fit(module: &LeanModule, table: &Table, columns: &[String], cursor: bool) -> ModelIndexFit {
  1155	    let declared = model_declared_indexes(module, table, columns, cursor);
  1156	    if declared.len() > 1 { return ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous }
  1157	    let selected = declared.first().copied();
  1158	    let physical = selected.map_or(columns, |index| index.columns.as_slice());
  1159	    let fits = physical.len() <= 8 && model_index_label_fits(module, table, physical, selected) && physical.iter().fold(PORTABLE_ID_BYTES, |bytes, column| {
  1160	        bytes.saturating_add(table.col(column).map_or(usize::MAX, |kind| model_column_bytes(&kind)))
  1161	    }) <= PORTABLE_KEY_BYTES;
  1162	    if fits { ModelIndexFit::Fits } else { ModelIndexFit::Nonportable }
  1163	}
  1164	
  1165	fn model_selected_index_fit(module: &LeanModule, table: &Table, name: &str) -> ModelIndexFit {
  1166	    let Some(decl) = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|decl| decl.name == table.name) else { return ModelIndexFit::Nonportable };
  1167	    let Some(index) = decl.indexes.iter().find(|index| index.name == name && index.is_plain()) else { return ModelIndexFit::Nonportable };
  1168	    let fits = index.columns.len() <= 8
  1169	        && model_index_label_fits(module, table, &index.columns, Some(index))
  1170	        && index.columns.iter().fold(PORTABLE_ID_BYTES, |bytes, column| {
  1171	            bytes.saturating_add(table.col(column).map_or(usize::MAX, |kind| model_column_bytes(&kind)))
  1172	        }) <= PORTABLE_KEY_BYTES;
  1173	    if fits { ModelIndexFit::Fits } else { ModelIndexFit::Nonportable }
  1174	}

  1176	fn model_read_by_form(rule: &Expr, pins: &[(String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
  1177	    let pinned = |column: &str| pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)| (name == column).then_some(*value));
  1178	    let viewer = |value: &Expr| matches!(value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer");
  1179	    match rule {
  1180	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "allow" => true,
  1181	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" =>
  1182	            model_read_by_form(left, pins) || model_read_by_form(right, pins),
  1183	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" =>
  1184	            model_read_by_form(left, pins) && model_read_by_form(right, pins),
  1185	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => {
  1186	            let (column, value) = match (column_of(left), column_of(right)) {
  1187	                (Some(column), None) => (column, right.as_ref()),
  1188	                (None, Some(column)) => (column, left.as_ref()),
  1189	                _ => return false,
  1190	            };
  1191	            match (value, pinned(column)) {
  1192	                (value, Some(pin)) if viewer(value) => viewer(pin),
  1193	                (Expr::Literal { value, .. }, Some(Expr::Literal { value: pin, .. })) =>
  1194	                    value == pin && !matches!(value, Literal::Null),
  1195	                _ => false,
  1196	            }
  1197	        }
  1198	        _ => false,
  1199	    }
  1200	}

  1202	fn model_independent(expression: &Expr) -> bool {
  1203	    match expression {
  1204	        Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Name { .. } => true,
  1205	        Expr::Member { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => model_independent(base),
  1206	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => model_independent(left) && model_independent(right),
  1207	        Expr::Call { args, .. } | Expr::Array { items: args, .. } => args.iter().all(model_independent),
  1208	        _ => false,
  1209	    }
  1210	}

  1212	fn model_equality_column(table: &Table, expression: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
  1213	    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = expression && op == "=" {
  1214	        if let Some(column) = column_of(left) && model_independent(right) && table.col(column).is_some() { return Some(column.into()) }
  1215	        if let Some(column) = column_of(right) && model_independent(left) && table.col(column).is_some() { return Some(column.into()) }
  1216	    }
  1217	    let value = if let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = expression && op == "not" { value.as_ref() } else { expression };
  1218	    let column = column_of(value)?;
  1219	    matches!(table.col(column), Some(Kind::Bool)).then(|| column.into())
  1220	}
  1221	
  1222	fn model_pin_columns(table: &Table, expressions: &[&Expr], order: &[String]) -> Vec<String> {
  1223	    expressions.iter().filter_map(|expression| model_equality_column(table, expression))
  1224	        .filter(|column| column != "id" && !order.contains(column)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().collect()
  1225	}

  1227	fn model_lane_columns(mut pins: Vec<String>, order: &[String]) -> Vec<String> {
  1228	    for column in order {
  1229	        if !pins.contains(column) { pins.push(column.clone()); }
  1230	    }
  1231	    if !pins.iter().any(|column| column == "id") { pins.push("id".into()); }
  1232	    pins
  1233	}

  1235	fn party_column<'a>(table: &Table, expression: &'a Expr) -> Option<&'a str> {
  1236	    let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = expression else { return None };
  1237	    if op != "=" { return None }
  1238	    let viewer = |value: &Expr| matches!(value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer");
  1239	    let column = if viewer(left) { column_of(right) } else if viewer(right) { column_of(left) } else { None }?;
  1240	    table.col(column).is_some().then_some(column)
  1241	}

  1243	fn membership_probe_kind(focal: &Table, expression: &Expr) -> Option<Kind> {
  1244	    match expression {
  1245	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Some(Kind::Id(IdSort::Principal)),
  1246	        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => focal.col(name),
  1247	        Expr::Literal { value, .. } => literal_scalar(value).ok()?.1,
  1248	        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-"
  1249	            && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(_), .. }) => Some(Kind::Int),
  1250	        _ => None,
  1251	    }
  1252	}

  1262	/// The physical source key and target parent column of one membership leaf.
  1263	/// A fully pinned total unique may replace the provisional discovery key; the
  1264	/// other fixed equalities are residuals on its at-most-one row. Otherwise an
  1265	/// applicable cap or the rule probe's total point admits metered discovery.
  1266	fn membership_physical(module: &LeanModule, focal: &Table, expression: &Expr) -> Option<ModelMembershipPhysical> {
  1267	    let Expr::Exists { read, .. } = expression else { return None };
  1268	    if read.range.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || read.keys.is_empty() { return None }
  1269	    let source = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
  1270	    let kinds = read.keys.iter().map(|key| membership_probe_kind(focal, &key.value)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  1271	    // Resolve from surface-declared unique keys and surface expression types,
  1272	    // independently of whatever plan the Rust binder selected. `Table::uniques`
  1273	    // deliberately excludes synthesized indexes.
  1274	    let columns = resolve_key(source, &read.keys, &kinds).ok()?;
  1275	    let mut fixed_terms = Vec::new();
  1276	    let mut target = None;
  1277	    for (key, column) in read.keys.iter().zip(&columns) {
  1278	        if let Some(parent) = column_of(&key.value) {
  1279	            if target.replace((column.clone(), parent.to_owned())).is_some() { return None }
  1280	        } else if !reads_row(&key.value) {
  1281	            // A fixed source id remains an exact witness constraint, but PAGE
  1282	            // evaluates it as a residual. It is neither a discovery prefix nor
  1283	            // a cap grouping term; physical indexes always append id last.
  1284	            if column != "id" { fixed_terms.push((column.clone(), &key.value)); }
  1285	        } else {
  1286	            return None;
  1287	        }
  1288	    }
  1289	    let (source_parent, target_parent) = target?;
  1290	    let bounded = source.caps.iter().any(|(_, by)|
  1291	        by.iter().all(|column| column != "id" && fixed_terms.iter().any(|term| &term.0 == column)
  1292	            && !source.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && col.on_delete == Some("setNull"))));
  1293	    let mut fixed = fixed_terms.iter().map(|term| term.0.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  1294	    fixed.sort();
  1295	    fixed.dedup();
  1296	    let source_parent_is_id = source_parent == "id";
  1297	    let surface = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == source.name);
  1298	    let point_total = surface.is_some_and(|table| table.indexes.iter().any(|index|
  1299	        model_total_unique(source, index)
  1300	            && read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|name| name == &index.name)
  1301	            && index.columns.iter().all(|column| columns.contains(column))));
  1302	    let read_rule = surface.and_then(|table| {
  1303	        let rules = table.rules.iter().filter(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  1304	        match rules.as_slice() { [rule] => Some(&rule.value), _ => None }
  1305	    });
  1306	    let one_row = surface.and_then(|table| table.indexes.iter().filter(|index| {
  1307	        let point = fixed_terms.iter().filter(|term| index.columns.contains(&term.0)).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
  1308	        model_total_unique(source, index)
  1309	            && index.columns.iter().all(|column| fixed.contains(column))
  1310	            && read_rule.is_some_and(|rule| model_read_by_form(rule, &point))
  1311	    }).min_by_key(|index| (index.columns.len(), &index.name)));
  1312	    let (source_key, source_index, source_admissible) = if let Some(index) = one_row {
  1313	        (index.columns.clone(), Some(index.name.clone()), true)
  1314	    } else {
  1315	        let mut source_key = fixed;
  1316	        if !source_key.contains(&source_parent) { source_key.push(source_parent); }
  1317	        if !source_key.iter().any(|column| column == "id") { source_key.push("id".into()); }
  1318	        // The ordinary path remains valid when `id` is a physical next component.
  1319	        // Only a reused declared index that omits it needs the one-row total-unique
  1320	        // proof; zero candidates means the planner synthesizes `source_key`, while
  1321	        // multiple candidates are rejected separately as ambiguous.
  1322	        let candidates = model_declared_indexes(module, source, &source_key, false);
  1323	        let total_id_source = !source_parent_is_id || match candidates.as_slice() {
  1324	            [index] => index.columns.iter().any(|column| column == "id") || model_total_unique(source, index),
  1325	            [] | [_, _, ..] => true,
  1326	        };
  1327	        (source_key, None, total_id_source && (bounded || point_total))
  1328	    };
  1329	    Some(ModelMembershipPhysical { source: source.name.clone(), columns: source_key, index: source_index, target_parent, source_admissible })
  1330	}

   673	  | _, _ => false

   675	def scopedWitnessDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl)

   693	        point == key && (sch.table? t).any fun source =>

   711	    (fs : List Expr) : Option (List (List Expr)) :=
   712	  let common := fs.filter (!·.isOr)

   714	  if factors.isEmpty then none else
   715	    let branches := factors.foldl (fun product factor =>
   716	      product.flatMap fun prior => factor.map fun alternative => prior ++ alternative) [[]]

   724	def branchDecided (sch : Schema) (t : String) (pins : List (String × Expr))

   774	    -- membership descriptors. Dynamic discovery does not count against
   775	    -- eight; its static membership descriptor does.

   797	def keyValue (k : Key) : Value := .arr (k.map .s)
   798	
   799	def parseCursor (order : List String) : Value → EM (Option Key)
   800	  | .s .null => pure none
   801	  | .arr xs =>
   802	    match xs.mapM (fun x => match x with | .s y => some y | _ => none) with
   803	    | some k => if k.length = order.length + 1 then pure (some k) else refuse "E_INPUT"
   804	    | none => refuse "E_INPUT"
   805	  | _ => refuse "E_INPUT"
   806	
   807	/-- A point read: the visible row with this id or unique key, or null; a
   808	null key component returns no row. -/
   809	def pointRead (vw : View) (t : String) (ks : List (String × Scalar)) : Value :=
   810	  if ks.any (·.2 == .null) then .null
   811	  else match (vw t).find? (keyMatch ks) with
   812	    | some r => r.toValue
   813	    | none => .null
   814	
   815	def markIncomplete : EM Unit := fun rs => .ok ((), { rs with complete := false })
   816	
   817	def markIf (b : Bool) : EM Unit := if b then markIncomplete else pure ()
   818	
   819	/-- A cut scalar read: unavailable in a query, `E_BOUND` in a mutation. -/
   820	def cutScalar {α : Type} (cx : Ctx) : EM α :=
   821	  if cx.mutation then refuse "E_BOUND" else throw .unavailable
   822	
   823	def pageAnswer (order : List String) (w : Walk) : Value × Option Value :=
   824	  let next := match w.stop with
   825	    | .exhausted => none
   826	    | _ => w.last.map fun r => keyValue (rowKey order r)
   827	  (.arr (w.rows.map Row.toValue), some (next.getD .null))
   828	
   829	/-- Whether any of `rs` passes the filter. -/
   830	def anyMatch (f : Row → EM Bool) : List Row → EM Bool
   831	  | [] => pure false
   832	  | r :: rs => do if ← f r then pure true else anyMatch f rs
   833	
   834	/-- After a full page, whether `next` is kept: always, except that a merged
   835	page keeps it only when a matching row follows. -/
   836	def lookahead (filt : Row → EM Bool) (order : List String) (desc : Bool) (merged : Bool) (w : Walk)
   837	    (cs : List Row) : EM Bool :=
   838	  if merged && w.stop = .filled then anyMatch filt (afterCursor order desc (w.last.map (rowKey order)) cs)
   839	  else pure true
   840	
   841	/-- A page: walk the candidates; a cut page is incomplete (and refuses
   842	`E_BOUND` in a mutation); a page whose read rule is not decided (by its pins,
   843	a readable grant, the pins' form or its branches) is never complete; `next`
   844	follows the last examined row unless the walk exhausted the candidates (reads
   845	card; LLP 3000.007 §5.6). A `merged` page (the merge of its branches) carries
   846	`next` after a full page only when another *matching* row follows: the merge
   847	knows each branch's next matching row (LLP 3000.036.001 §5). -/

   850	    (filt : Row → EM Bool) (order : List String) (desc : Bool) (take : Nat) (cur : Option Key)

codex
The product recognizers use the same top-level conjunction shape, and the validator checks each pair’s condition, assignment target, previous-result link, and empty else branch. I’m tracing how the emitter counts descriptors and prices keys, and how vacancy authorization cuts reach the page’s completeness result.

   126	            if lane.system() {

   128	                let prefix = self.clone_branch_prefix(prefix, metered_merge)?;
   129	                let opened = self.open_branch_lane(lane, prefix, after.as_ref(), None, metered_merge)?;

   131	            }

   133	                let prior = self.env.get_mut(&lane.binding).map(|slot| std::mem::replace(slot, Value::Null));

   138	                        else {
   139	                            self.meter.heap(word_heap::text(&lane.binding) + word_heap::insertion::<String, Value>(self.env.len()))?;
   140	                            self.env.insert(lane.binding.clone(), value);

   145	                        // source-field copies are allocated. The full bound

   246	    fn open_branch_lane<'a>(&mut self, lane: &'a MergeLane, prefix: Vec<Value>, after: Option<&(Vec<Value>, bool)>, grant: Option<super::super::expiry::Pending>, metered: bool) -> Result<Lane<'a>, RuntimeError> {
   247	        let scan = &lane.scan;
   248	        let definition = &self.schema.tables[&scan.table]; let index = &definition.indexes[&scan.index];
   249	        // A branch cursor was validated once above. Do not clone the compiled
   250	        // Scan merely to clear it; evaluate only the remaining bounds here.
   251	        let mut bounds = self.word_bounds(scan, definition, index, None, metered, true)?;
   252	        if metered && let Some((values, _)) = after { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(values).heap())?; }
   253	        let point_id = rules::membership_point_id(self.schema, lane);
   254	        let mut done = false;
   255	        if point_id {
   256	            // The point's pinned id is also its logical page position. Decide
   257	            // the shared cursor here instead of applying it after the fully
   258	            // consumed physical prefix.
   259	            if let Some((values, inclusive)) = after {
   260	                let position = &prefix[prefix.len() - 1..];
   261	                let comparison = Self::compare(position, values, scan.order);
   262	                done = comparison == Ordering::Less || comparison == Ordering::Equal && !inclusive;
   263	            }
   264	        } else {
   265	            match after { Some((values, true)) => bounds.at_or_after = Some(values.clone()), Some((values, false)) => bounds.after = Some(values.clone()), None => {} }
   266	        }
   267	        let suffix = index.components.iter().skip(scan.prefix.len()).filter_map(|component| component.key_name());
   268	        let (position_len, ends_in_id) = suffix.fold((0usize, false), |(len, _), name| (len + 1, name == "id"));
   269	        let position_len = position_len + usize::from(!ends_in_id || point_id);
   270	        Ok(Lane { scan, prefix, bounds, buffer: VecDeque::new(), done, grant, vacancy: lane.vacancy(), metered, position_len, through: None })
   271	    }

   273	    /// Read a lane's next batch strictly after its position, recording how
   274	    /// far it examined.
   275	    fn fill_branch(&mut self, lane: &mut Lane<'_>, batch: usize) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   276	        if lane.done { return Ok(()); }
   277	        // Arm the capture with the lane's own table and index: `scan_consumed`
   278	        // then records the last rule-visible candidate this read examined,
   279	        // filter rejects included (never a nested read's key).
   280	        if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::text(&lane.scan.table) + word_heap::text(&lane.scan.index))?; }
   281	        let prior = self.scan_frontier.replace((lane.scan.table.clone(), lane.scan.index.clone(), None));
   282	        let rows = self.read_branch(lane, batch);
   283	        let examined = std::mem::replace(&mut self.scan_frontier, prior).and_then(|(_, _, key)| key);
   284	        let rows = rows?;
   285	        let capped = self.read_capped();
   286	        lane.done = rows.len() < batch && !capped;
   287	        let mut read_to = None;
   288	        if capped && let Some(key) = examined {
   289	            if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::key_decode(lane.prefix.len() + lane.position_len, key.len()))?; }
   290	            if let Some(mut values) = crate::key::decode(&key) {
   291	                if lane.prefix.len() <= values.len() { values.drain(..lane.prefix.len()); }
   292	                values.truncate(lane.position_len);
   293	                if values.len() == lane.position_len { read_to = Some(values); }
   294	            }
   295	        }
   296	        if read_to.is_none() && let Some(row) = rows.last() {
   297	            self.charge_branch_position(lane, row)?;
   298	            read_to = Some(self.branch_position(lane, row));
   299	        }
   300	        if read_to.is_some() { lane.through = read_to; }
   301	        lane.buffer = rows.into();
   302	        Ok(())
   303	    }

   305	    /// The position every unfinished lane has examined through: the least in
   306	    /// the page's order, or `None` when some lane examined nothing past the
   307	    /// cursor. A finished lane bounds nothing.
   308	    fn frontier_index(lanes: &[Lane<'_>], order: Order) -> Option<usize> {
   309	        let mut least: Option<usize> = None;
   310	        for (position, lane) in lanes.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, lane)| !lane.done) {
   311	            let through = lane.through.as_ref()?;
   312	            if least.is_none_or(|n| Self::compare(through, lanes[n].through.as_ref().expect("frontier"), order) == Ordering::Less) { least = Some(position); }
   313	        }
   314	        least
   315	    }

   317	    fn compare(a: &[Value], b: &[Value], order: Order) -> Ordering {
   318	        let ordering = a.iter().zip(b).find_map(|(x, y)| match x.total_cmp(y) { Ordering::Equal => None, other => Some(other) }).unwrap_or(a.len().cmp(&b.len()));
   319	        if order == Order::Asc { ordering } else { ordering.reverse() }
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    /// `key` is at or before `through` in the page's order.
   323	    fn within(key: &[Value], through: Option<&Vec<Value>>, order: Order) -> bool {
   324	        through.is_some_and(|through| Self::compare(key, through, order) != Ordering::Greater)
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    /// One read of a lane strictly after its current position. A lane its
   328	    /// grant admitted reads as a decided page (`scan_consumed`'s grant arm).
   329	    fn read_branch(&mut self, lane: &Lane<'_>, batch: usize) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   330	        self.merge_grant = lane.grant;
   331	        self.merge_vacancy = lane.vacancy;
   332	        if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(&lane.prefix).heap() + DisclosedBounds(&lane.bounds).heap())?; }
   333	        let result = self.scan_consumed(&lane.scan, lane.prefix.clone(), lane.bounds.clone(), batch, None, None, false, lane.metered);
   334	        self.merge_grant = None;
   335	        self.merge_vacancy = false;
   336	        Ok(result?.0)
   337	    }

   339	    fn branch_prefix_values(&mut self, scan: &Scan, metered: bool) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   340	        if metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::slots::<Value>(scan.prefix.len()))?; }
   341	        let mut values = Vec::with_capacity(scan.prefix.len());
   342	        for key in &scan.prefix {
   343	            values.push(match key {
   344	                IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) => self.expr(expr)?,
   345	                IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_) => return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a branch prefix is never a words prefix".into())),
   346	            });
   347	        }
   348	        Ok(values)
   349	    }

   351	    fn clone_branch_prefix(&mut self, prefix: &[Value], metered: bool) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   352	        if metered { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(prefix).heap())?; }
   353	        Ok(prefix.to_vec())
   354	    }
   355	
   356	    fn push_branch_lane<'a>(&mut self, lanes: &mut Vec<Lane<'a>>, lane: Lane<'a>, metered: bool, site: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   357	        if metered {
   358	            self.meter.at(site);
   359	            if !self.read_charge(MeterKind::Steps, 1)? { return Ok(()); }
   360	            word_heap::push(&mut self.meter, lanes, lane)

+interpretations: fixed residual point guards and exact vacancy lanes. A device
+that only understands `branch-pages-v3` must refuse this IR before decoding or
+executing it; bounded products reuse the already validated positive-branch

+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — older devices refuse PAGE5's validator
+// interpretation instead of executing fixed residual or vacancy lanes as v3.
+#[test]
+fn page5_plans_require_branch_pages_v4_from_the_device() {
+    let vacancy = "table holds:
+  item: designs
+  owner: principal
+  unique item
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+table designs:
+  owner: principal
+  read <- not exists holds[item = .id] or .owner = viewer
+  online only
+query available(c: cursor ?):
+  return designs first 1 by .id after c
+";
+    let fixed = "table roles:
+  bucket: int
+  principal: principal
+  unique bucket, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table access:
+  item: items
+  principal: principal
+  unique item, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table items:
+  bucket: int
+  at: int
+  read <- exists roles[.bucket, viewer] and exists access[.id, viewer]
+  online only
+query page(bucket: int, c: cursor ?):
+  return items where .bucket = bucket first 1 by .at after c
+";
+    let product = "table items:
+  owner: principal
+  helper: principal
+  status: text <=10
+  at: int
+  read <- (.owner = viewer or .helper = viewer) and (.status = 'open' or .status = 'active')
+  online only
+query page(c: cursor ?):
+  return items first 1 by .at after c
+";
+    for (name, source) in [("fixed", fixed), ("vacancy", vacancy)] {
+        let (_, backend) = compile(source);
+        assert!(backend.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"), "{name}");
+        let required = snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&backend.schema.public_projection());
+        let mut old = snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine::current();
+        old.version = "0.4.28-page4".into();
+        old.capabilities.retain(|capability| capability != "branch-pages-v4");
+        let refusal = snapback4_core::device_protocol::check(Some(&old), &required).unwrap_err();
+        assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_DEVICE_VERSION", "{name}");
+        assert!(refusal.message.contains("branch-pages-v4"), "{name}: {refusal:?}");
+    }
+    let (_, product_backend) = compile(product);
+    assert!(!product_backend.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"),
+        "bounded products reuse the existing branch validator");
+}
+

+/// PAGE5's three new planner families are generated independently so their
+/// evidence does not depend on the broader widening stream selecting a page.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — bounded products, fixed+varying grants and exact vacancy all receive generated cases.
+pub fn page5(seed: u64, shape: u64) -> Case {
+    let mut case = base(seed);
+    let mut rng = Rng::new(seed ^ 0x5041_4745_355f_454e ^ shape);
+    case.program.body = Body::Read { kind: Read::Page, take: 1 + rng.pick(3) as u32,
+        descending: rng.pick(2) == 0, filter: false, owner: false, project: None };
+    case.program.prelude.clear(); case.steps.clear(); case.program.lets.clear();
+    case.program.order = Some(vec!["id".into()]); case.program.named_page_order = None;
+    case.program.principal_arg = None; case.program.residual = None; case.program.guard = None;
+    case.program.search_term = None; case.program.membership_named_reordered = false;
+    case.program.boolean_grant_normalized = false; case.program.kids = None;
+    case.program.tables[0].effects = None; case.program.tables[0].residency = Residency::Online;
+    let bucket = case.args["bucket"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
+    match shape % 3 {
+        0 => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::ProductScope;
+            case.program.domain = Some("(.owner = viewer or .helper = viewer) and (.status = 'open' or .status = 'active')".into());
+            let items = &mut case.program.tables[0];
+            if !items.columns.iter().any(|column| column.name == "helper") { items.columns.push(column("helper", Type::Principal)); }
+            if !items.columns.iter().any(|column| column.name == "status") { items.columns.push(column("status", Type::Text)); }
+            for (table, row) in &mut case.rows { if table == "items" {
+                row["helper"] = json!(if rng.pick(2) == 0 { "alice" } else { "bob" });
+                row["status"] = json!(["open", "active", "closed"][rng.pick(3) as usize]);
+            }}
+        }
+        1 => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::FixedPerRow;
+            case.program.domain = Some(".bucket = bucket".into());
+            case.program.order = Some(vec!["score".into()]);
+            id_membership(&mut case);
+            let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+            access.rule = Rule::Member; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Online;
+            access.expire = None; access.not_before = None; access.caps.clear();
+            let roles = Table { name: "roles".into(), columns: vec![column("bucket", Type::Int), column("principal", Type::Principal)],
+                keys: vec![("byBucketPrincipal".into(), vec!["bucket".into(), "principal".into()], true)], caps: vec![],
+                rule: Rule::Member, residency: Residency::Online, effects: None, expire: None, not_before: None };
+            case.program.tables.push(roles);
+            if rng.pick(4) != 0 { case.rows.push(("roles".into(), json!({"id":format!("role-{seed}"), "bucket":bucket, "principal":case.principal}))); }
+        }
+        _ => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::Vacancy;
+            case.program.domain = Some("true".into());
+            id_membership(&mut case);
+            let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+            access.rule = Rule::Member; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Online;
+            access.expire = None; access.not_before = None; access.caps.clear();
+            access.keys.retain(|(name, _, _)| name == "byItem");
+            access.keys.iter_mut().for_each(|(_, columns, unique)| { *columns = vec!["item".into()]; *unique = true; });
+            let mut items = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+            case.rows.retain(|(table, row)| table != "access" || items.insert(row["item"].to_string()));
+        }
+    }
+    case
+}
+
+pub(crate) fn page5_features(case: &Case) -> Vec<String> {
+    let family = match case.program.tables[0].rule {
+        Rule::ProductScope => Some("product-scope"),
+        Rule::FixedPerRow => Some("fixed-per-row"),
+        Rule::Vacancy => Some("vacancy"),
+        _ => None,
+    };
+    family.map(|name| vec![format!("page5/{name}")]).unwrap_or_default()
+}

+    let result = engine.query("page", context("alice", &[("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("c", Value::Null)])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    assert_eq!(result.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("visible"));
+
+    let vacancy = bound("table holds:
+  item: designs
+  owner: principal
+  unique item
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+table designs:
+  owner: principal
+  read <- not exists holds[item = .id] or .owner = viewer
+  online only
+query available(c: cursor ?):
+  return designs first 1 by .id after c
+");
+    assert!(vacancy.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(vacancy.schema, vacancy.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("holds".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("hold")), ("item", id("held")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("free")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("held")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("available", context("bob", &[("c", Value::Null)])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    assert_eq!(result.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("free"));
+}
 // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — only live memberships may synthesize PAGE target lanes.

 fn owner_or_membership_page_ignores_an_expired_unswept_witness() {
diff --git a/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean b/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean
index 88bb1a8455..f53c348985 100644
--- a/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean
+++ b/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean
@@ -568,6 +568,35 @@ def laneFits (order : List String) (eqs pins : List (String × Expr)) : Bool :=
 def laneDecided (td : TableDecl) (order : List String) (eqs pins : List (String × Expr)) : Bool :=
   laneFits order eqs pins && decidedPins td ((eqs ++ pins).filter fun p => p.1 != "id" && !order.contains p.1)
 
+/-- PAGE5's fixed half of a fixed-plus-varying grant: after substituting the
+page pins, this is one timeless, required exact point whose ordinary read
+rule discloses the same answer to the caller. -/
+def fixedPointGuard (sch : Schema) (outer : TableDecl)
+    (pins : List (String × Expr)) : Rule → Bool
+  | r@(.probe t point key) => match sch.table? t with
+    | some source =>
+      let columns := key.map (·.1)
+      key.length == point.length
+        && point.eraseDups.length == point.length
+        && point.all columns.contains
+        && (point == ["id"] || source.uniques.contains point)
+        && point.all (fun name => name == "id" || source.cols.any (fun col => col.name == name && !col.nullable))
+        && source.notBefore.isNone && source.expire.isNone
+        && r.usableGuard sch outer pins
+    | none => false
+  | _ => false
+
+/-- PAGE5's varying half has exactly one outer-row key component and otherwise
+uses PAGE's existing readable, total membership witness. -/
+def varyingPointGrant (sch : Schema) (pins : List (String × Expr))
+    (order : List String) : Rule → Bool
+  | .probe t point key => membershipEligible sch t point key
+      && (membershipColumns key).any fun pair =>
+        !pins.any (·.1 == pair.2)
+          && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])
+          && laneFits order [(pair.2, .viewer)] pins
+  | _ => false
+
 /-- Each positive alternative either has a prefix-decided rule, its own
 readable guard, or one exact membership witness with every remaining
 conjunct true by its pins. -/
@@ -593,6 +622,9 @@ def compoundDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl) (pins : List (String × Expr
                   !ps.any (·.1 == pair.2) && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])
                   && laneFits order ((pair.2, .viewer) :: extra) pins
               | _ => false
+            | [a, b] =>
+                (fixedPointGuard sch td ps a && varyingPointGrant sch ps order b)
+                  || (fixedPointGuard sch td ps b && varyingPointGrant sch ps order a)
             | _ => false)
 
 
@@ -621,6 +653,17 @@ def scopedWitnessDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl)
               && laneFits order [(pair.2, .viewer)] pins
     | _ => false
 
+/-- PAGE5's explicit vacancy: a top-level negated probe of one required
+exact point by the focal row's intrinsic id. The native lane still checks the
+whole read rule per row; this predicate governs only its exhaustion verdict. -/
+def vacancyDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl) : Bool :=
+  td.read.disjuncts.any fun r => match r with
+    | .not (.probe t [point] [(key, .col .self "id")]) =>
+        point == key && (sch.table? t).any fun source =>
+          source.uniques.contains [point]
+            && (source.cols.find? (·.name == point)).all (!·.nullable)
+    | _ => false
+
 /-- The pins the single-index planner keeps: the page's equalities and bare
 boolean conjuncts, except `id` and an order column other than the first,
 which stay seeks (`Requests::read`; GPT round 3, finding 3). -/
@@ -705,7 +748,8 @@ def branchDecided (sch : Schema) (t : String) (pins : List (String × Expr))
       | none => false
     let case4 := guards && !reservedMembership && !pinnedMembership && alts.isNone && compoundDecided sch td pins order
     let witnessScope := guards && pins.all (fun p => p.2.total) && scopedWitnessDecided sch td pins fs order
-    (total && (case1 || case2)) || (readFree && (case3 || case4)) || witnessScope
+    let vacancy := guards && pins.isEmpty && fs.isEmpty && vacancyDecided sch td
+    (total && (case1 || case2)) || (readFree && (case3 || case4)) || witnessScope || vacancy
 
 def keyValue (k : Key) : Value := .arr (k.map .s)

+def page5Product : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "items", cols := [c "owner", c "helper", c "status", c "at"]
+    read := .and
+      (.or (.eq (self "owner") .viewer) (.eq (self "helper") .viewer))
+      (.or (.eq (self "status") (.lit (T "open"))) (.eq (self "status") (.lit (T "active")))) }] }
+
+def page5FixedPerRow : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "roles", cols := [c "bucket", c "principal"], uniques := [["bucket", "principal"]]
+    read := .eq (self "principal") .viewer },
+  { name := "access", cols := [c "item", c "principal"], uniques := [["item", "principal"]]
+    read := .eq (self "principal") .viewer },
+  { name := "items", cols := [c "bucket", c "at"]
+    read := .and
+      (.probe "roles" ["bucket", "principal"] [("bucket", self "bucket"), ("principal", .viewer)])
+      (.probe "access" ["item", "principal"] [("item", self "id"), ("principal", .viewer)]) }] }
+
+def page5Vacancy : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "holds", cols := [c "design", c "owner"], uniques := [["design"]], read := .eq (self "owner") .viewer },
+  { name := "designs", cols := [c "owner", c "at"],
+    read := .or (.not (.probe "holds" ["design"] [("design", self "id")])) (.eq (self "owner") .viewer) }] }
+def page5VacancyExamples : List Example := [
+  { name := "page5-bounded-product"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: two principal pins times two status pins retain both predicates in four complete lanes"
+    case := {
+      schema := page5Product
+      store := mkStore [("items", [
+        mkRow "a" [("owner", I "alice"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "open"), ("at", N 1)],
+        mkRow "b" [("owner", I "bob"), ("helper", I "alice"), ("status", T "active"), ("at", N 2)],
+        mkRow "c" [("owner", I "alice"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "closed"), ("at", N 3)],
+        mkRow "d" [("owner", I "bob"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "open"), ("at", N 4)]])]
+      viewer := alice
+      program := query [.ret (.page "items" [] none ["at"] false 10 none)] }
+    tables := []
+    expected := r#"{"kind":"answer","data":[{"id":"a","owner":"alice","helper":"bob","status":"open","at":1},{"id":"b","owner":"bob","helper":"alice","status":"active","at":2}],"complete":true,"next":null}"# },
+  { name := "page5-fixed-plus-per-row"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: one readable fixed point may guard one varying per-row membership lane"
+    case := {
+      schema := page5FixedPerRow
+      store := mkStore [
+        ("roles", [mkRow "r" [("bucket", N 1), ("principal", I "alice")]]),
+        ("access", [mkRow "x" [("item", I "a"), ("principal", I "alice")],
+          mkRow "y" [("item", I "c"), ("principal", I "alice")]]),
+        ("items", [mkRow "a" [("bucket", N 1), ("at", N 1)],
+          mkRow "b" [("bucket", N 1), ("at", N 2)],
+          mkRow "c" [("bucket", N 2), ("at", N 3)]])]
+      viewer := alice
+      program := query [.ret (.page "items" [("bucket", int 1)] none ["at"] false 10 none)] }
+    tables := []
+    expected := r#"{"kind":"answer","data":[{"id":"a","bucket":1,"at":1}],"complete":true,"next":null}"# },
+  { name := "page5-exact-vacancy"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: explicit exact-point vacancy makes private server exhaustion observable"
+    case := {
+      schema := page5Vacancy
+      store := mkStore [("holds", [mkRow "h" [("design", I "held"), ("owner", I "alice")]]),
+        ("designs", [mkRow "free" [("owner", I "alice"), ("at", N 1)],
+          mkRow "held" [("owner", I "alice"), ("at", N 2)]])]
+      viewer := bob
+      program := query [.ret (.page "designs" [] none ["at"] false 10 none)] }
+    tables := []
+    expected := r#"{"kind":"answer","data":[{"id":"free","owner":"alice","at":1}],"complete":true,"next":null}"# }
+]
+

-/// A membership PAGE with two independent `or` filters stays on the ordinary

-// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — undistributed query alternatives are outside the constructive PAGE fragment.
+/// A membership PAGE whose alternative product pins the membership's varying

@@ -543,3 +549,34 @@ pub fn page_membership_filter_fallback(seed: u64) -> Case {

+
+/// Two independent equality-alternative factors are a bounded Cartesian
+/// membership plan when no alternative pins the membership's varying field.
+/// This mirrors the native planner in the independent Lean classifier.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — bounded positive query products retain every factor in each lane.
+pub fn page_membership_filter_product(seed: u64) -> Case {
+    let mut case = base(seed);
+    let mut rng = Rng::new(seed ^ 0x5041_4745_355f_5052);
+    case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::Witness;
+    case.program.tables[0].effects = None;
+    case.program.tables[0].residency = Residency::Online;
+    case.program.tables[0].columns.push(column("region", Type::Text));
+    case.program.tables[0].columns.push(column("status", Type::Text));
+    let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+    access.rule = Rule::Allow; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Public;
+    access.expire = None; access.not_before = None;
+    case.program.body = Body::Read { kind: Read::Page, take: 1 + rng.pick(3) as u32,
+        descending: rng.pick(2) == 0, filter: false, owner: false, project: None };
+    case.program.domain = Some("(.region = 'north' or .region = 'south')".into());
+    case.program.residual = Some("(.status = 'open' or .status = 'held')".into());
+    case.program.order = Some(vec!["label".into(), "score".into()]);
+    case.program.named_page_order = None; case.program.prelude.clear(); case.program.lets.clear();
+    case.steps.clear();
+    for (table, row) in &mut case.rows {
+        if table == "items" {
+            row["region"] = json!(if rng.pick(3) == 0 { "other" } else if rng.pick(2) == 0 { "north" } else { "south" });
+            row["status"] = json!(if rng.pick(3) == 0 { "closed" } else if rng.pick(2) == 0 { "open" } else { "held" });
+        }
+    }
+    case
+}

--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-difftest/tests/smoke.rs

         }
     }
 }
+
+#[test]
+fn membership_page_with_two_equality_alternative_factors_is_complete_in_lean_and_native() {
+    let have_model = model_built();
+    for seed in 0..4 {
+        let case = snapback4_difftest::generate::page_membership_filter_product(seed);
+        let source = case.program.source();
+        execute::compile(&source).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!("seed {seed}: {error}\n{source}"));
+        let mut engine = execute::engine(&case, &source).unwrap();
+        let outcome = execute::execute(&mut engine, &case, "run", &case.args);
+        assert_eq!(outcome.value["complete"], true, "seed {seed}: {outcome:?}\n{source}");
+        let mut report = Report::default(); campaign::check(&case, &mut report, |_| {});
+        assert!(!report.failed(), "seed {seed}: {report:?}\n{source}");
+        if have_model {
+            let checked = snapback4_difftest::lean::compare(&case, &source).unwrap();
+            assert!(matches!(checked.verdict, Verdict::Pass), "seed {seed}: {:?}\n{source}", checked.verdict);
+        }
+    }
+}

     2	use super::catalog::Catalog;
     3	use crate::diag::Diagnostic;
     4	use snapback4_core::{Value, key, rows::{MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES, MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES}, schema::{ColumnType, IndexComponent, Schema}};
     5	
     6	// identity_fits permits at most 135 bytes after JSON escaping; key escaping
     7	// is no larger. Include the key tag and two-byte terminator, even for byId
     8	// (the physical layout appends the identity a second time).
     9	const ID_BYTES: usize = 138;
    10	
    11	fn column_bytes(kind: &ColumnType) -> usize {
    12	    match kind {
    13	        ColumnType::Optional(inner) => column_bytes(inner),
    14	        ColumnType::Id | ColumnType::Principal | ColumnType::Ref { .. } => ID_BYTES,
    15	        ColumnType::Text { max } => (*max as usize).saturating_mul(4).saturating_add(3),
    16	        ColumnType::Bytes { max } => (*max as usize).saturating_mul(2).saturating_add(3),
    17	        ColumnType::Enum { variants } => variants.iter().map(|v| key::encode(&[Value::String(v.clone())]).len()).max().unwrap_or(1),
    18	        ColumnType::Bool => 1,
    19	        ColumnType::Int | ColumnType::IntRange { .. } | ColumnType::Decimal { .. } | ColumnType::Money { .. } | ColumnType::Time => 9,
    20	        // The existing schema validator refuses these in an index.
    21	        _ => usize::MAX,
    22	    }
    23	}
    24	
    25	/// Optional branch planning must not introduce a later key-bound refusal.
    26	/// Use the same column sizes and appended identity as the schema check.
    27	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#3 — oversized branch indexes leave the original read intact
    28	pub(super) fn plain_index_fits(table: &super::catalog::TableInfo, index: &crate::ast::IndexDecl, emitted_name: &str) -> bool {
    29	    table.decl.name.len().saturating_add(emitted_name.len()) <= MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES
    30	        && index.words.is_none() && index.computed.is_empty()
    31	        && index.columns.iter().fold(ID_BYTES, |bytes, column| {
    32	            let size = table.columns.get(column).map(|ty| column_bytes(&crate::types::scalar_to_column(ty)))
    33	                .unwrap_or(if column == "id" { ID_BYTES } else { usize::MAX });
    34	            bytes.saturating_add(size)
    35	        }) <= MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES
    36	}
    37	
    38	// @ref LLP 3000.009#4.3 — bound components, encoding overhead and the id.
    39	pub(super) fn check(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    40	    for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
    41	        for (index_name, index) in &table.indexes {
    42	            let site = catalog.site_for(&format!("tables.{name}.indexes.{index_name}"));
    43	            let generated = index_name.starts_with("$intent:");
    44	            let origin = if generated {
    45	                let unique = catalog.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|table| table.decl.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.name == *index_name && index.unique));
    46	                format!("{} at {site}", if unique { "unique declaration" } else { "access required by the declaration or read" })
    47	            } else { format!("index {name}.{index_name}") };
    48	            if name.len().saturating_add(index_name.len()) > MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES {
    49	                let rewrite = if generated { "shorten the table or column names to leave space for the portable encoded key" } else { "shorten the table or index name to leave space for the portable encoded key" };
    50	                diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&site, "E_INDEX_KEY_BYTES", format!("table and index labels for {origin} exceed {MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES} combined UTF-8 bytes"), rewrite).guide("indexes"));
    51	            }
    52	            let mut components = Vec::new();
    53	            for component in &index.components {
    54	                match component {
    55	                    IndexComponent::Column(column) => {
    56	                        if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(column) { components.push((column.clone(), column_bytes(kind))); }
    57	                    }
    58	                    IndexComponent::Calendar(key) => components.push((format!("{}({})", key.function, key.column), 9)),
    59	                    IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { .. } => components.push(("words(..)".into(), snapback4_core::words::MAX_TOKEN_BYTES + 3)),
    60	                    IndexComponent::Contribution(maintain) => {
    61	                        for column in &maintain.by {
    62	                            let bytes = if maintain.computed.contains_key(column) { 9 } else { table.columns.get(maintain.aliases.get(column).unwrap_or(column)).map_or(usize::MAX, column_bytes) };
    63	                            components.push((column.clone(), bytes));
    64	                        }
    65	                    }
    66	                }
    67	            }
    68	            let total = components.iter().fold(ID_BYTES, |n, (_, size)| n.saturating_add(*size));
    69	            if total <= MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES { continue; }
    70	            let (column, size) = components.iter().max_by_key(|(_, n)| n).cloned().unwrap_or(("id".into(), ID_BYTES));
    71	            let rest = total.saturating_sub(size);
    72	            let chars = MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES.saturating_sub(rest).saturating_sub(3) / 4;
    73	            let alternative = if generated { String::new() } else { format!(", or remove components from {index_name}") };
    74	            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&site, "E_INDEX_KEY_BYTES",
    75	                format!("{origin} can require {total} encoded bytes; column {name}.{column} exceeds the portable {MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES}-byte key budget including the id"),
    76	                format!("bound {column}, for example `{column}: text <={chars}`{alternative}; all components and the appended id must fit {MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES} bytes")).guide("indexes"));
    77	        }
    78	    }
    79	}

    99	        assert!(result.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_INDEX_KEY_BYTES"));
   100	    }
   101	    #[test]
   102	    fn bound_diagnostic_survives_an_independent_schema_error_and_links_indexes() {
   103	        let source = "table items:\n  a: text <=500\n  by byA: a\n  public 'test'\n  online only\ntable broken:\n  by missing: nonexistent\n  public 'test'\n  online only\n";
   104	        let result = crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
   105	        assert!(result.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_INDEX_KEY_BYTES"), "{:?}", result.diagnostics);
   106	        assert!(result.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code != "E_INDEX_KEY_BYTES"));
   107	        assert_eq!(crate::diag::guide_for("E_INDEX_KEY_BYTES"), "indexes");
